# OTA-FIXED-SKY130: electrical and measurement contract

OTA-FIXED-SKY130 is the current fixed-topology transistor sizing, bias, and compensation task.

All analyses use the pinned SKY130 combined/continuous model family. The calibrated absolute follower-error limit is **5 mV**. The registered task packet, specification JSON, initial design, bounds, testbench, evaluation matrix, and source hashes define the executable experiment. A participant's results cannot change the scoring rules for its registered campaign. Machine filenames and IDs are retained for exact hash matching.

## Circuit and public domain

Optimize the documented 14 variables of a two-stage, single-ended OTA. The input devices and load are symmetric, `nf=1`, and `IREF=10 µA`. `Cc` is 0.1–5 pF, `Rz` is 0–50 kΩ, width is at most 100 µm, length is at least 0.15 µm, and total Σ(W×L) is at most 2,000 µm². Every device must remain in the frozen OTA-FIXED-SKY130 W/L domain in `bounds.json`. The evaluator uses native ngspice and SKY130 1.8 V normal-VT NFET/PFET models. Topology changes are outside the task.

The specified load interval is continuous from 2 to 5 pF. Public exploration uses 2 and 5 pF. The nine process, supply, and temperature conditions are:

| ID | Corner | VDD | Temperature |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| N | TT | 1.80 V | 27 °C |
| SLC | SS | 1.62 V | −40 °C |
| SLH | SS | 1.62 V | 125 °C |
| SHC | SS | 1.98 V | −40 °C |
| SHH | SS | 1.98 V | 125 °C |
| FLC | FF | 1.62 V | −40 °C |
| FLH | FF | 1.62 V | 125 °C |
| FHC | FF | 1.98 V | −40 °C |
| FHH | FF | 1.98 V | 125 °C |

| Scored requirement | Limit |
|---|---:|
| Low-frequency loop gain | ≥60 dB |
| Unity-gain frequency | ≥15 MHz |
| Phase margin | ≥60° |
| Power | ≤600 µW |
| Maximum DC tracking error | ≤2 mV |
| Small-step overshoot | ≤10% |
| Large-step 20–80% slew | ≥10 V/µs |
| Small-step settling | Within 1% by 100 ns |
| Large-step settling | Within 1% by 200 ns |
| Input-referred integrated noise, 10 Hz–10 MHz | ≤100 µV RMS |
| PSRR+ at 1 kHz / 1 MHz | ≥60 dB / ≥24 dB |
| Open-loop CMRR at 1 kHz | ≥50 dB |
| Deterministic robustness pass fraction | ≥27/30 within the frozen absolute follower-error limit |

Gain margin and statistical quiescent power are characterization metrics. The absolute follower-error limit is **5 mV**, selected before independent statistical verification. Functional limits are checked under the continuous models.

## Testbench and measurements

The follower connects `vout` to `vin_n`, with `vin_p` as the non-inverting input. VSS is 0 V and VCM is 0.9 V. An ideal source injects 10 µA from ground into `ibias`, with AC magnitude zero. Participants cannot edit the PDK, loads, sources, testbench, or evaluator. Solver settings are `reltol=1e-4`, `abstol=1e-13`, `vntol=1e-9`, and `chgtol=1e-16`.

**Operating point and DC.** At each load, evaluate input levels 0.75, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0, and 1.05 V. Power is the net power entering the DUT from VDD, bias, input, and feedback ports, not VDD alone. Score the maximum over the complete DC sweep only when every required point is valid. Device operating points at the five input levels are extracted from that same sweep; they incur no extra launches. Sweep the follower from 0.75 to 1.05 V on a 5 mV grid and take the largest absolute input/output difference. Keep per-load results distinct from load-independent aggregates.

**Loop response.** Insert a series source in feedback with DC zero and AC magnitude one. Use `L = −(1 + 1/V(vin_n))`. The verification band is 10 Hz–1 GHz with 20 points per decade. All samples must be finite, frequencies strictly increasing, endpoints within relative error `1e-7`, and adjacent log10 frequency spacing at most `0.050001`. Missing values, non-finite values, or inadequate coverage are `INVALID_DATA`.

Interpolate linearly in log10 frequency and save every unity crossing, touch, and boundary event. The first valid positive-to-negative gain crossing sets UGF. Unwrap phase from the lowest-frequency principal phase, limiting each adjacent change to ±180°. Phase margin is `180° + unwrapped phase` at the selected UGF. Save phase and margin at every crossing. The stored field `phase_margin_deg_worst` contains the selected crossing's margin; its selection metadata makes that meaning explicit.

After the selected UGF, any saved gain sample above `0 dB + 1e-6 dB` fails `no_0db_recross`. Do not infer an unseen curve shape between samples. No valid UGF or non-finite PM cannot pass. Save all odd-180° phase crossings and calculate GM as negative loop gain there. Complete in-band data with no phase crossing receives `NO_PHASE_CROSSING_IN_BAND`, distinct from missing data. A phase crossing is never required merely to create a GM number.

**Transient response.** Evaluate both directions of the small step 0.8↔1.0 V and large step 0.75↔1.05 V. Hold the initial level for 100 ns, use a 1 ns edge, and observe through 2.1 µs with a maximum 25 ps output step. Measure settling from the edge start. The band is 1% of the input step and is centered on that PVT/load's independently solved destination DC output. Record the interpolated final entry that stays within the band until the end. Score DC tracking separately; a transient tail sample does not replace the DC solution. If a complete waveform stays outside the band, settling is null with the observation time saved as a lower bound; a bound past the limit is a MISS. Overshoot remains measurable even if settling fails.

Overshoot is the largest excursion past target divided by the absolute independent DC output transition. Only small-step overshoot is scored; large-step overshoot is retained as characterization. Large-step slew uses the first ordered, direction-correct 20% and 80% crossings after the edge ends. Retain later recrossings. Missing, non-finite, short, or target-less waveforms are measurement failures, not convenient electrical values.

**Noise.** Save output noise ASD referred to the input using the same circuit's input gain. Integrate its squared PSD by the trapezoid rule in frequency, interpolating band boundaries, then take the square root. The native sweep is 10 Hz–10 MHz at 50 points per decade. Keep PSD points and the integrals from 10, 100, and 1,000 Hz. The lower cutoff by itself does not establish the 1/f contribution.

**PSRR+.** In the same DC follower, measure `Hsupply` with VDD AC=1 and input AC=0, and `Hsignal` with VDD AC=0 and input AC=1. Both AC sweeps run in one ngspice process. VCM=0.9 V and IREF=10 µA do not follow supply ripple. Input-referred PSRR+ is `20 log10(|Hsignal/Hsupply|)`. Read 1 kHz and 1 MHz directly and save intermediate sweep data. Do not convert a zero transfer to an automatic infinite PASS. Score at N, SLH, and FHC with 2 and 5 pF loads.

**CMRR.** Use the natural open-loop DC operating point with both inputs fixed at **0.9 V**, independent of VDD. Differential excitation is Vin+ AC=0.5∠0° and Vin− AC=0.5∠180°, giving Vdiff=1 V. Common excitation is AC=1∠0° on both inputs, giving Vcm=1 V. Compute Ad=Vout/Vdiff, Acm=Vout/Vcm, and CMRR=20 log10(abs(Ad/Acm)) at exactly 1 kHz. Each native case bundles both AC sweeps and the DC operating point. Retain both raw AC paths, input phasors and OP data. Zero transfers, invalid OP and nonfinite values are measurement failures. Score N, SLH and FHC at 2 and 5 pF. The native 24-case calibration and independent amplitude-normalization check support 50 dB.

**Statistical follower error.** The scored deck places `.option seed` before `.lib ".../combined/continuous/sky130.lib.spice" mc`, then explicitly sets `.param MC_MM_SWITCH=1`. Use a unity-gain follower, VDD=1.80 V, 27 °C, CL=2 pF, Vin+=0.9 V and IREF=10 µA. For every sample retain convergence/status, Vout, signed `follower_error_v=Vout−0.9`, its absolute value and signed-port quiescent power. This is **closed-loop follower offset/error**, not an exact intrinsic Vos measurement. Both `MC_PR_SWITCH=1` and `MC_MM_SWITCH=1` are measured in the native deck. Following Analog Design Bench, the unmodified native model defines this deterministic test. Its process expressions are re-evaluated per reference by the pinned ngspice; a common-die global random shift has **not** been validated. The score is a fixed-seed native-model robustness test, with no silicon-yield interpretation.

Participants may explore only the four diagnostic seeds **52001–52004**. Each records one native case within the 90-minute model-call exploration window; there is no exploration case limit. CMRR exploration is available normally. Independent verification occurs only after the submitted DUT is frozen; results are withheld from the participant.

# OTA-FIXED-SKY130

Specification identity: `sha256:7fe3858aa141dd82698d855bbe7c74bec7b70d5ba2cf2c13774eeba72609e88b`

Optimize the existing two-stage SKY130 OTA by changing only the 14 parameters in
design.json. Read CLIENT.md, measurement.md, bounds.json, spec.json and the shared baseline.
The baseline contains continuous-model measurements at N, SLH and FHC; its scope
and unmeasured quantities are stated explicitly. Topology, IREF=10 µA and VCM=0.9 V are fixed.

Budget: 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. All native launches, including failures, are recorded. CLIENT.md
lists every operation, its required fields and measurement case costs. Use help
for the same machine-readable contract and estimate for a free case-count check.
Public PVT conditions are in conditions.json; exploration loads are 2 and 5 pF.

Only edit the params object in design.json; retain every other field unchanged.
Use unique experiment IDs for different measurements or designs. You do not need
to fill in predictions, confidence ratings or per-experiment interpretations.
Brief explanations of major design decisions in ordinary responses are useful
and are recorded automatically. Submit explicitly with a rationale when finished.

Signoff scores CMRR and deterministic robustness as well as the functional
requirements. Robustness requires at least 27 passing valid samples out of 30
independent fixed samples. Invalid samples fail. The metric is closed-loop follower
offset/error; it is not an intrinsic Vos measurement or a silicon yield estimate.
The absolute error limit is in spec.json. An unset limit indicates an unqualified
preview packet that cannot start a campaign.

Submission freezes the DUT. Independent verification occurs after your session ends;
its results are unavailable for redesign. Use only this packet and the provided
client; other runs, references, developer files and external tools are outside scope.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# Participant client

Run `atb-client --request REQUEST.json`. `atb-client status` returns remaining time and cases.
`atb-client help` prints the task operation contract; it costs no native cases. Requests and
exact responses are recorded by the host, including errors. Work only with this
run's packet and client. Electrical limits and measurement definitions are in
measurement.md and spec.json.

Commands: `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
`atb-client help` lists the task's analyses and case costs. `--cl` uses farads.

## Operations

Each line below is a complete JSON request that can be saved to REQUEST.json.

```json
{"action":"help"}
{"action":"status"}
{"action":"estimate","analysis":"step","pvt":"N","cl":5e-12}
{"action":"validate"}
{"action":"measure","experiment_id":"e001","analysis":"loop","pvt":"N","cl":2e-12}
{"action":"measure","experiment_id":"mm001","analysis":"mismatch","pvt":"N","cl":2e-12,"seed":52001}
{"action":"submit","rationale":"Explain why you chose this measured design and any known limitations."}
```

- help and status are available after submission too. All other actions require
  an open participant session. End the session after successful submission.
- estimate accepts analysis, pvt, optional cl and optional seed. It plans the
  exact native cases without a solver launch or budget reservation. It does not
  check a design or guarantee that budget will remain available later.
- validate, measure and submit automatically attach /workspace/design.json.
  Edit only its params object. Geometry is in micrometres without unit suffixes;
  cc and rz accept the documented SPICE suffixes.
- measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt. cl is optional; omitted cl
  uses 2 pF. Request 5 pF explicitly for the other public load.
- experiment_id is 1 to 64 letters, digits, underscores or hyphens. A completed
  identical request with the same design returns the saved reply for free. Use
  a new ID whenever conditions or design change. An interrupted request must
  not be replayed; preserve the error and continue within the remaining budget.
- seed is allowed only for mismatch, at N and 2 pF, using 52001 through 52004.
- No intent, target, hypothesis, prediction, confidence or interpretation fields
  are needed. There is no separate annotation operation. Ordinary responses are
  captured automatically; do not spend time discovering undocumented actions.
- submit requires a nonempty rationale. It freezes the DUT. Independent results
  are withheld. A saved design without explicit submission is not scored.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 7 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-loop-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_up-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_down-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_up-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_down-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-noise-cl2e-12 |
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 7 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-loop-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_up-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_down-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_up-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_down-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-noise-cl5e-12 |
| op | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl2e-12 |
| op | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl5e-12 |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl2e-12 |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl5e-12 |
| loop | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-loop-cl2e-12 |
| loop | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-loop-cl5e-12 |
| step | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 5 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_up-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_down-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_up-cl2e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_down-cl2e-12 |
| step | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 5 | ota-sky130-dcsweep-5mV-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_up-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-small_down-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_up-cl5e-12, ota-sky130-step-large_down-cl5e-12 |
| noise | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-noise-cl2e-12 |
| noise | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-noise-cl5e-12 |
| psrr | N, SLH, FHC | 2e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-psrr-cl2e-12 |
| psrr | N, SLH, FHC | 5e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-psrr-cl5e-12 |
| cmrr | N, SLH, FHC | 2e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-cmrr-cl2e-12 |
| cmrr | N, SLH, FHC | 5e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-cmrr-cl5e-12 |
| mismatch | N | 2e-12 | 1 | ota-sky130-mismatch-seed52001 |

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- op: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- loop: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- step: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- noise: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- cmrr: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
- mismatch: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. Required diagnostic seed: 52001, 52002, 52003, 52004.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.


A step request includes one DC sweep containing all destination operating points
and four transient cases per load. Nominal adds loop and noise for seven cases;
it does not include PSRR, CMRR or mismatch. Shared work inside a nominal request
is counted once. The table comes from the same planner used by measurement.
Failed simulations consume their reservation. help, status, estimate and validate
launch no simulator. Reply fields include ok, experiment_id, revision, cases_reserved,
solver_launches, cases, summary and budget. A failed request returns ok=false and
an error or execution details; missing measurements are not passing results.

# Baseline

Initial design at N, SLH and FHC with both endpoint loads. Includes full-range DC tracking and signed-port power, device operating points, loop response, four transients, noise, PSRR and CMRR. Other conditions and robustness are not included. See baseline.json.
----
* ATB OTA-SKY130 DUT -- two-stage Miller-compensated OTA on SKY130 1.8 V devices
*
* Topology: M1/M2 are the NMOS differential pair with common source ntl.
* M3 is the diode-connected PMOS load at na; M4 mirrors it into n1.  M7 is
* the PMOS second stage (source vdd, gate n1, drain vout), loaded by the
* NMOS current sink M8 (drain vout, source vss).  Diode-connected Mbn accepts
* ibias; its gate also biases the NMOS tail device Mtl and M8.
* Miller compensation connects n1 to vout through Cc and optional series Rz.
* For rz = 0, render_dut connects Cc directly to vout and omits Rz.
* The netlist below is authoritative.  docs/figures/ota-sky130.svg and the
* editable project in circuits/ota-sky130/schematic/ are derived documentation,
* not sizing or evaluation sources.
*
* Static polarity (large-signal, checked in bench tb_dc):
*   A rise on vin_p raises the M2 current and lowers the M4 current; node n1
*   falls; the PMOS M7 gate falls, so M7 sources more current and vout rises.
*   Hence vin_p is the NON-inverting input and vin_n (gate of M1, whose drain
*   is the diode-connected node na) is the inverting input of the complete
*   two-stage amplifier.
*
* SKY130 devices are PDK subcircuits, so every device below is an X instance.
* Body terminals are tied to the supply rails; there is no separate bulk port
* in P0.  The DUT contains no behavioural source.
*
.subckt atb_ota_sky130 vin_p vin_n vout vdd vss ibias
XMbn ibias ibias vss  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={{wbn}} l={{lbn}} nf=1
XMtl ntl   ibias vss  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={{wtl}} l={{ltl}} nf=1
XM1  na    vin_n ntl  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={{win}} l={{lin}} nf=1
XM2  n1    vin_p ntl  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={{win}} l={{lin}} nf=1
XM3  na    na    vdd  vdd sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w={{wld}} l={{lld}} nf=1
XM4  n1    na    vdd  vdd sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w={{wld}} l={{lld}} nf=1
XM7  vout  n1    vdd  vdd sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w={{wp2}} l={{lp2}} nf=1
XM8  vout  ibias vss  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w={{wn2}} l={{ln2}} nf=1
{{cc_net}}
.ends atb_ota_sky130

Inspect the W/L bin domain per device type and corner

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
b=json.load(open('bounds.json'))
bins=b['bins']
for dev in bins:
    for corner in bins[dev]:
        lst=bins[dev][corner]
        print(dev, corner, len(lst))
# Are bins identical across corners?
for dev in bins:
    ref=bins[dev]['tt']
    for corner in bins[dev]:
        print(dev, corner, 'same as tt:', bins[dev][corner]==ref)
# Print the unique bins for n tt and p tt in compact form
for dev in bins:
    print('==', dev)
    for x in bins[dev]['tt']:
        print(x)

Inspect baseline.json structure

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
b=json.load(open('baseline.json'))
def show(o, depth=0, maxdepth=3, prefix=''):
    if isinstance(o, dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            s = json.dumps(v)
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)) and depth<maxdepth:
                print('  '*depth + f'{k}: ({type(v).__name__}, {len(v)} items, {len(s)} chars)')
                show(v, depth+1, maxdepth)
            else:
                print('  '*depth + f'{k}: ' + s[:200])
    elif isinstance(o, list):
        print('  '*depth + f'[list of {len(o)}]; first: ' + json.dumps(o[0])[:200] if o else '[]')
show(b, 0, 2)
EOF

Inspect N/2pF baseline structure

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
b=json.load(open('baseline.json'))
n=b['electrical']['N']['loads']['2e-12']
def show(o, depth=0, maxdepth=4):
    if isinstance(o, dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            s = json.dumps(v)
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)) and depth<maxdepth:
                print('  '*depth + f'{k}: ({type(v).__name__}, {len(v)} items, {len(s)} chars)')
                show(v, depth+1, maxdepth)
            else:
                print('  '*depth + f'{k}: ' + s[:160])
    elif isinstance(o, list):
        print('  '*depth + f'[list of {len(o)}]; first: ' + (json.dumps(o[0])[:160] if o else ''))
show(n, 0, 3)

# find psrr / cmrr
def find(o, path=''):
    if isinstance(o, dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            if 'psrr' in k.lower() or 'cmrr' in k.lower():
                print(path+'/'+k, type(v).__name__, len(json.dumps(v)))
            find(v, path+'/'+k)

0:06:05

Each case takes about 1 s, so a full-PVT evaluation is cheap. I'll write an evaluation kit that sets design params, runs all measurements in parallel, and parses margins against the spec.

ACTION
0:06:31
Write · evalkit.py
/workspace/runs/evalkit.py
content
import json, os, subprocess, time, math, threading
import concurrent.futures as cf

WS = '/workspace'
DESIGN = f'{WS}/design.json'
RUNS = f'{WS}/runs'
RAW = f'{RUNS}/raw'
os.makedirs(RAW, exist_ok=True)

PVTS = ['N', 'SLC', 'SLH', 'SHC', 'SHH', 'FLC', 'FLH', 'FHC', 'FHH']
SPEC3 = ['N', 'SLH', 'FHC']
LOADS = [2e-12, 5e-12]
SEEDS = [52001, 52002, 52003, 52004]
KEYS = ['wbn', 'lbn', 'wtl', 'ltl', 'win', 'lin', 'wld', 'lld', 'wp2', 'lp2', 'wn2', 'ln2', 'cc', 'rz']

_lock = threading.Lock()


def fmt_params(p):
    """p: dict of floats (cc in F, rz in ohm, geometry in um) -> design.json string params."""
    out = {}
    for k in KEYS:
        v = p[k]
        if k == 'cc':
            out[k] = f'{v * 1e12:.4g}p'
        elif k == 'rz':
            out[k] = f'{v:.5g}'
        else:
            out[k] = f'{v:.4g}'
    return out


def parse_params(sp):
    """design.json string params -> floats"""
    def conv(s):
        s = str(s).strip().lower()
        mult = {'p': 1e-12, 'n': 1e-9, 'u': 1e-6, 'k': 1e3, 'f': 1e-15, 'm': 1e-3, 'meg': 1e6}
        for suf in ['meg', 'p', 'n', 'u', 'k', 'f', 'm']:
            if s.endswith(suf):
                return float(s[:-len(suf)]) * mult[suf]
        return float(s)
    return {k: conv(sp[k]) for k in KEYS}


def write_design(p):
    d = json.load(open(DESIGN))
    d['params'] = fmt_params(p)
    json.dump(d, open(DESIGN, 'w'), indent=2)
    return d['params']


def gate_area(p):
    return (p['wbn'] * p['lbn'] + p['wtl'] * p['ltl'] + 2 * p['win'] * p['lin']
            + 2 * p['wld'] * p['lld'] + p['wp2'] * p['lp2'] + p['wn2'] * p['ln2'])


def run_req(req, tag=None):
    eid = req['experiment_id']
    path = f'{RAW}/{eid}.json'
    if os.path.exists(path):
        try:
            return json.load(open(path))
        except Exception:
            pass
    rp = f'{RAW}/req_{eid}.json'
    json.dump(req, open(rp, 'w'))
    for attempt in range(1):
        try:
            cp = subprocess.run(['atb-client', '--request', rp], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
            out = cp.stdout
            try:
                r = json.loads(out)
            except Exception:
                r = {'ok': False, 'error': 'unparseable', 'stdout': out[:2000], 'stderr': cp.stderr[:2000]}
        except Exception as e:
            r = {'ok': False, 'error': 'exception %r' % (e,)}
    if r.get('ok'):
        json.dump(r, open(path, 'w'))
    else:
        json.dump(r, open(f'{RAW}/FAILED_{eid}.json', 'w'))
    return r


def make_requests(tag, nominal=True, special=True, mismatch=True, pvts=PVTS, loads=LOADS, seeds=SEEDS):
    reqs = []
    if nominal:
        for pvt in pvts:
            for cl in loads:
                reqs.append(('nom', pvt, cl, None, {'action': 'measure', 'experiment_id': f'{tag}_nom_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}p',
                                                    'analysis': 'nominal', 'pvt': pvt, 'cl': cl}))
    if special:
        for pvt in SPEC3:
            if pvt not in pvts and not special == 'all':
                pass
            for cl in loads:
                for an in ['psrr', 'cmrr']:
                    reqs.append((an, pvt, cl, None, {'action': 'measure', 'experiment_id': f'{tag}_{an}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}p',
                                                     'analysis': an, 'pvt': pvt, 'cl': cl}))
    if mismatch:
        for s in seeds:
            reqs.append(('mm', 'N', 2e-12, s, {'action': 'measure', 'experiment_id': f'{tag}_mm_{s}', 'analysis': 'mismatch',
                                               'pvt': 'N', 'cl': 2e-12, 'seed': s}))
    return reqs


def evaluate(tag, p, workers=8, **kw):
    """Runs all requested measurements for the design p. Returns dict of raw replies keyed (kind,pvt,cl,seed)."""
    with _lock:
        write_design(p)
        reqs = make_requests(tag, **kw)
        t0 = time.time()
        res = {}
        with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex:
            futs = {ex.submit(run_req, r[4]): r for r in reqs}
            for f in cf.as_completed(futs):
                r = futs[f]
                res[(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3])] = f.result()
    return res


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- parsing
def g(d, *ks, default=None):
    for k in ks:
        if d is None:
            return default
        try:
            d = d[k]
        except Exception:
            return default
    return d


def parse_nominal(r, cl):
    """Return flat metrics dict from a nominal reply."""
    m = {}
    if not r.get('ok'):
        m['fail'] = True
        return m
    s = r['summary']
    ck = repr(cl) if repr(cl) in s['loop'] else str(cl)
    # find load key
    lk = None
    for k in s['loop']:
        if abs(float(k) - cl) < 1e-15:
            lk = k
    lp = s['loop'][lk]
    m['gain_db'] = lp.get('loop_gain_db_at_ref')
    m['ugf'] = lp.get('selected_unity_gain_hz')
    m['pm'] = lp.get('phase_margin_deg_worst')
    m['recross'] = lp.get('no_0db_recross')
    m['gm_db'] = lp.get('gain_margin_db_worst')
    for lab in ['small_up', 'small_down', 'large_up', 'large_down']:
        st = None
        for k, v in s['step'].items():
            if k.startswith(lab + '|') and abs(float(k.split('|')[1]) - cl) < 1e-15:
                st = v
        if st is None:
            m[f'{lab}_status'] = 'missing'
            continue
        m[f'{lab}_valid'] = st.get('valid')
        m[f'{lab}_settle'] = st.get('settling_time_s')
        m[f'{lab}_settle_lb'] = st.get('settling_time_lower_bound_s')
        m[f'{lab}_os'] = st.get('overshoot_ratio')
        m[f'{lab}_slope'] = st.get('large_step_20_80_slope_v_per_s')
    nz = None
    for k, v in s['noise'].items():
        if abs(float(k) - cl) < 1e-15:
            nz = v
    m['noise'] = g(nz, 'input_noise_rms_v')
    m['dc_err'] = s.get('dc_tracking_error_v_max')
    m['power'] = s.get('quiescent_power_w_worst')
    # DC op detail at five inputs (first load)
    try:
        dcs = s['dc_sweeps']
        for k, v in dcs.items():
            if abs(float(k) - cl) < 1e-15:
                m['dc_by_vin'] = {vin: op.get('tracking_error_v') for vin, op in v['op_points'].items()}
                m['op'] = v['op_points']
    except Exception:
        pass
    return m


def parse_psrr(r):
    if not r.get('ok'):
        return {'fail': True}
    c = r['cases'][0]['measurements']
    if c.get('status') != 'OK':
        return {'fail': True}
    f = c['frequencies']
    return {'psrr1k': f['1000']['psrr_plus_input_referred_db'], 'psrr1M': f['1000000']['psrr_plus_input_referred_db']}


def parse_cmrr(r):
    if not r.get('ok'):
        return {'fail': True}
    c = r['cases'][0]['measurements']
    if c.get('status') != 'OK':
        return {'fail': True}
    return {'cmrr': c['cmrr_db'], 'ol_vout': c['open_loop_op']['vout_v']}


def parse_mm(r):
    if not r.get('ok'):
        return {'fail': True}
    c = r['cases'][0]['measurements']
    if c.get('status') != 'OK' or c.get('convergence') != 'OK':
        return {'fail': True}
    return {'err': c['follower_error_v'], 'vout': c['vout_v'], 'p': c['quiescent_power_w']}


def collect(res):
    """Flatten raw replies -> dict: metrics[(kind,pvt,cl)] etc."""
    out = {'nom': {}, 'psrr': {}, 'cmrr': {}, 'mm': {}}
    for (kind, pvt, cl, seed), r in res.items():
        if kind == 'nom':
            out['nom'][(pvt, cl)] = parse_nominal(r, cl)
        elif kind == 'psrr':
            out['psrr'][(pvt, cl)] = parse_psrr(r)
        elif kind == 'cmrr':
            out['cmrr'][(pvt, cl)] = parse_cmrr(r)
        elif kind == 'mm':
            out['mm'][seed] = parse_mm(r)
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- margins
def margins(out):
    """Normalized margins (>=0 passes). Returns dict name -> (margin, raw value)"""
    M = {}

    def put(name, marg, raw):
        M[name] = (marg, raw)

    for (pvt, cl), m in out['nom'].items():
        tag = f'{pvt}/{int(cl*1e12)}p'
        if m.get('fail'):
            put(f'FAIL nominal {tag}', -5.0, None)
            continue
        v = m.get('gain_db')
        put(f'gain {tag}', -5 if v is None else (v - 60) / 10, v)
        v = m.get('ugf')
        put(f'ugf {tag}', -5 if v is None else v / 15e6 - 1, v)
        v = m.get('pm')
        put(f'pm {tag}', -5 if v is None else (v - 60) / 30, v)
        v = m.get('recross')
        put(f'recross {tag}', -5 if v != 1 else 1.0, v)
        for lab in ['small_up', 'small_down']:
            v = m.get(f'{lab}_os')
            put(f'os_{lab} {tag}', -5 if v is None else (0.1 - v) / 0.1, v)
            t = m.get(f'{lab}_settle')
            put(f'ts_{lab} {tag}', -5 if t is None else (100e-9 - t) / 100e-9, t)
        for lab in ['large_up', 'large_down']:
            v = m.get(f'{lab}_slope')
            put(f'slew_{lab} {tag}', -5 if v is None else v / 10e6 - 1, v)
            t = m.get(f'{lab}_settle')
            put(f'ts_{lab} {tag}', -5 if t is None else (200e-9 - t) / 200e-9, t)
        v = m.get('noise')
        put(f'noise {tag}', -5 if v is None else 1 - v / 100e-6, v)
        v = m.get('dc_err')
        put(f'dcerr {pvt}', -5 if v is None else 1 - v / 2e-3, v)
        v = m.get('power')
        put(f'power {pvt}', -5 if v is None else 1 - v / 600e-6, v)
    for (pvt, cl), m in out['psrr'].items():
        tag = f'{pvt}/{int(cl*1e12)}p'
        if m.get('fail'):
            put(f'FAIL psrr {tag}', -5.0, None)
            continue
        put(f'psrr1k {tag}', (m['psrr1k'] - 60) / 20, m['psrr1k'])
        put(f'psrr1M {tag}', (m['psrr1M'] - 24) / 10, m['psrr1M'])
    for (pvt, cl), m in out['cmrr'].items():
        tag = f'{pvt}/{int(cl*1e12)}p'
        if m.get('fail'):
            put(f'FAIL cmrr {tag}', -5.0, None)
            continue
        put(f'cmrr {tag}', (m['cmrr'] - 50) / 20, m['cmrr'])
    for seed, m in out['mm'].items():
        if m.get('fail'):
            put(f'FAIL mm {seed}', -5.0, None)
            continue
        put(f'mm {seed}', 1 - abs(m['err']) / 5e-3, m['err'])
    return M


def summarize(M, topn=12):
    items = sorted(M.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1][0])
    worst = items[:topn]
    nfail = sum(1 for k, v in M.items() if v[0] < 0)
    return nfail, worst


def score(M):
    """Scalar objective: maximize. Penalize violations strongly, reward worst margin."""
    vals = [v[0] for v in M.values()]
    if not vals:
        return -99
    neg = sum(min(0, x) for x in vals)
    worst = min(vals)
    return worst + 0.2 * neg / max(1, len(vals)) ** 0.5

import sys, json, time
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from evalkit import *

BASE = {'wbn': 10, 'lbn': 1, 'wtl': 60, 'ltl': 1, 'win': 72, 'lin': 1.2, 'wld': 6, 'lld': 0.5,
        'wp2': 90, 'lp2': 0.5, 'wn2': 90, 'ln2': 1.2, 'cc': 4e-12, 'rz': 1200}


def show(tag, p, out, M, topn=14):
    nfail, worst = summarize(M, topn)
    print(f'== {tag}  area={gate_area(p):.1f}  fails={nfail}/{len(M)}  score={score(M):.3f}')
    print('params:', fmt_params(p))
    for k, (mg, raw) in worst:
        print(f'   {mg:8.3f}  {k:28s} {raw}')


def table(out):
    # compact per PVT/load table
    print('PVT/CL   gain   ugf(MHz)  pm   osSU  osSD  slewLU slewLD(V/us) tsSD(ns) noise(uV) dcerr(mV) P(uW)')
    for (pvt, cl), m in sorted(out['nom'].items(), key=lambda kv: (PVTS.index(kv[0][0]), kv[0][1])):
        if m.get('fail'):
            print(pvt, cl, 'FAIL')
            continue
        f = lambda x, s=1.0, n=1: ('%.*f' % (n, x * s)) if x is not None else 'None'
        print(f'{pvt:4s}{int(cl*1e12)}p {f(m["gain_db"])} {f(m["ugf"],1e-6)} {f(m["pm"])} {f(m["small_up_os"],100)} {f(m["small_down_os"],100)} '
              f'{f(m["large_up_slope"],1e-6)} {f(m["large_down_slope"],1e-6)} {f(m["small_down_settle"],1e9)} {f(m["noise"],1e6)} {f(m["dc_err"],1e3,2)} {f(m["power"],1e6,0)}')
    for k in ['psrr', 'cmrr']:
        for (pvt, cl), m in sorted(out[k].items(), key=lambda kv: (PVTS.index(kv[0][0]), kv[0][1])):
            print(k, pvt, int(cl * 1e12), {a: (round(b, 2) if isinstance(b, float) else b) for a, b in m.items()})
    for s, m in sorted(out['mm'].items()):
        print('mm', s, {a: (round(b * 1e3, 3) if a in ('err', 'vout') and isinstance(b, float) else b) for a, b in m.items()})


if __name__ == '__main__':
    tag = sys.argv[1]
    over = json.loads(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else {}
    p = dict(BASE)
    p.update(over)
    t0 = time.time()
    res = evaluate(tag, p)
    out = collect(res)
    M = margins(out)
    show(tag, p, out, M)
    table(out)
    print('elapsed %.1fs' % (time.time() - t0))
    json.dump({'params': p, 'margins': {k: v for k, v in M.items()},
               'out': {kk: {str(k): v for k, v in vv.items()} for kk, vv in out.items()}},
              open(f'{RUNS}/res_{tag}.json', 'w'), default=str)

Write · dcprobe.py
/workspace/runs/dcprobe.py
content
import sys, json, time
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from evalkit import *
from run_eval import BASE

_n = [0]


def dc_probe(tag, p, pvts=PVTS, cl=2e-12, verbose=True):
    write_design(p)
    rows = {}
    for pvt in pvts:
        req = {'action': 'measure', 'experiment_id': f'{tag}_op_{pvt}', 'analysis': 'op', 'pvt': pvt, 'cl': cl}
        r = run_req(req)
        if not r.get('ok'):
            rows[pvt] = {'fail': str(r)[:300]}
            continue
        s = r['summary']
        d = list(s['dc_sweeps'].values())[0]
        ops = d['op_points']
        row = {'maxerr_mV': d['dc_tracking_error_v_max'] * 1e3, 'P_uW': d['quiescent_power_w_worst'] * 1e6}
        for vin in ['0.750', '0.900', '1.050']:
            op = ops[vin]
            nv = op['node_voltages_v']
            dev = op['device_op']
            row[vin] = {'err_mV': op['tracking_error_v'] * 1e3, 'N1': nv['N1'], 'NA': nv['NA'], 'NTL': nv['NTL'],
                        'vds1': dev['M1']['VDS'], 'vds2': dev['M2']['VDS'], 'itail_uA': dev['MTL']['ID'] * 1e6,
                        'i7_uA': dev['M7']['ID'] * 1e6, 'gm1_uS': dev['M1'].get('GM', 0) * 1e6, 'gm7_uS': dev['M7'].get('GM', 0) * 1e6,
                        'vds7': dev['M7']['VDS'], 'vout': op['vout_v']}
        rows[pvt] = row
    if verbose:
        print(f'--- {tag} area={gate_area(p):.0f} {fmt_params(p)}')
        for pvt, row in rows.items():
            if 'fail' in row:
                print(pvt, 'FAIL', row['fail'])
                continue
            a, b, c = row['0.750'], row['0.900'], row['1.050']
            print(f"{pvt:4s} err={row['maxerr_mV']:7.2f}mV P={row['P_uW']:4.0f}uW | 0.9V: N1-NA={1e3*(b['N1']-b['NA']):6.1f}mV N1={b['N1']:.3f} NA={b['NA']:.3f} gm1={b['gm1_uS']:.0f} gm7={b['gm7_uS']:.0f} It={b['itail_uA']:.1f} I7={b['i7_uA']:.1f}"
                  f" | vds1(.75/.9/1.05)={a['vds1']:.3f}/{b['vds1']:.3f}/{c['vds1']:.3f} vds2={a['vds2']:.3f}/{b['vds2']:.3f}/{c['vds2']:.3f} NTL(.75)={a['NTL']:.3f} It(.75)={a['itail_uA']:.1f} vds7(1.05)={c['vds7']:.3f}")
    return rows


if __name__ == '__main__':
    tag = sys.argv[1]
    over = json.loads(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else {}
    pv = sys.argv[3].split(',') if len(sys.argv) > 3 else PVTS
    p = dict(BASE)
    p.update(over)
    dc_probe(tag, p, pv)

    if m.get('fail'): print(k,'FAIL'); continue
    f=lambda x,s=1.0,n=1:('%.*f'%(n,x*s)) if isinstance(x,(int,float)) else str(x)
    print(k, 'gain',f(m['gain_db']),'ugf',f(m['ugf'],1e-6),'pm',f(m['pm']),'osSU',f(m['small_up_os'],100),'osSD',f(m['small_down_os'],100),'tsSU',f(m['small_up_settle'],1e9),'tsSD',f(m['small_down_settle'],1e9),'slLU',f(m['large_up_slope'],1e-6),'slLD',f(m['large_down_slope'],1e-6),'tsLU',f(m['large_up_settle'],1e9),'tsLD',f(m['large_down_settle'],1e9),'nz',f(m['noise'],1e6),'P',f(m['power'],1e6,0))
EOF

Probe DC behaviour for larger load PMOS candidates

cat > probe_many.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from dcprobe import *

def compact(tag, over, pvts=PVTS):
    p = dict(BASE); p.update(over)
    rows = dc_probe(tag, p, pvts, verbose=False)
    print(f'## {tag} {json.dumps(over)}')
    for pvt in pvts:
        row = rows[pvt]
        if 'fail' in row:
            print(pvt, 'FAIL'); continue
        a, b, c = row['0.750'], row['0.900'], row['1.050']
        print(f"  {pvt:4s} err={row['maxerr_mV']:7.2f} P={row['P_uW']:4.0f} N1-NA(.9)={1e3*(b['N1']-b['NA']):6.1f} vds1@1.05={c['vds1']:.3f} vds1@.9={b['vds1']:.3f} Vtl@.75={a['NTL']:.3f} It(.75)={a['itail_uA']:.1f} I7={b['i7_uA']:.0f} gm1={b['gm1_uS']:.0f} gm7={b['gm7_uS']:.0f} vds7@1.05={c['vds7']:.3f} NA={b['NA']:.3f}")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    cands = json.loads(sys.argv[1])
    pv = sys.argv[2].split(',') if len(sys.argv) > 2 else PVTS
    for tag, over in cands.items():
        compact(tag, over, pv)
EOF

Run first batch of screening candidates in background

cat > screen.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json, hashlib, math, time
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from evalkit import *
from run_eval import BASE


def dhash(p):
    s = json.dumps(fmt_params(p), sort_keys=True)
    return hashlib.md5(s.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]


def meas(p, an, pvt, cl=2e-12, seed=None):
    write_design(p)
    eid = f"h{dhash(p)}_{an}_{pvt}_{int(round(cl*1e12*10))}" + (f"_{seed}" if seed else "")
    req = {'action': 'measure', 'experiment_id': eid, 'analysis': an, 'pvt': pvt, 'cl': cl}
    if seed:
        req['seed'] = seed
    return run_req(req)


def err09(p, pvt='N', cl=2e-12):
    r = meas(p, 'op', pvt, cl)
    m = r['cases'][0]['measurements']
    return m['op_points']['0.900']['tracking_error_v'] * 1e3, m


def tune(p, tol=0.08, maxit=4, key='wld'):
    for i in range(maxit):
        e, m = err09(p)
        if abs(e) < tol:
            break
        p[key] = float(f'{p[key] * math.exp(e / 0.9):.4g}')
    return p, e


def parse_step(r):
    out = {}
    for c in r['cases']:
        if c['bench'] != 'step':
            continue
        lab = c['case_id'].split('-step-')[1].split('-cl')[0]
        m = c['measurements']
        out[lab] = {'ts': m.get('settling_time_s'), 'os': m.get('overshoot_ratio'), 'slope': m.get('large_step_20_80_slope_v_per_s'),
                    'valid': m.get('valid'), 'lb': m.get('settling_time_lower_bound_s')}
    return out


def parse_loop(r):
    m = r['cases'][0]['measurements']
    return {'gain': m.get('loop_gain_db_at_ref'), 'ugf': m.get('selected_unity_gain_hz'), 'pm': m.get('phase_margin_deg_worst'),
            'recross': m.get('no_0db_recross')}


def fx(x, s=1.0, n=1):
    return ('%.*f' % (n, x * s)) if isinstance(x, (int, float)) else 'None'


def screen(tag, over, corners=(('N', 5e-12),), do_tune=True, pvts_dc=('N',), extra=True):
    p = dict(BASE)
    p.update(over)
    if do_tune:
        p, e = tune(p)
    h = dhash(p)
    line = f'## {tag} h={h} area={gate_area(p):.0f} ' + ' '.join(f'{k}={p[k]:.4g}' for k in KEYS)
    print(line)
    for pvt in pvts_dc:
        e, m = err09(p, pvt)
        o = m['op_points']['0.900']
        dv = m['dc_tracking_error_v_max'] * 1e3
        print(f"   op {pvt}: err0.9={e:.2f} maxerr={dv:.2f}mV P={m['quiescent_power_w_worst']*1e6:.0f}uW N1-NA={1e3*(o['node_voltages_v']['N1']-o['node_voltages_v']['NA']):.1f}mV "
              f"I7={o['device_op']['M7']['ID']*1e6:.0f} It={o['device_op']['MTL']['ID']*1e6:.0f}")
    for (pvt, cl) in corners:
        lp = parse_loop(meas(p, 'loop', pvt, cl))
        st = parse_step(meas(p, 'step', pvt, cl))
        s = lambda k, f: st[k][f]
        print(f"   {pvt}/{int(cl*1e12)}p gain={fx(lp['gain'])} ugf={fx(lp['ugf'],1e-6)} pm={fx(lp['pm'])} | osSU={fx(s('small_up','os'),100)} osSD={fx(s('small_down','os'),100)} "
              f"tsSU={fx(s('small_up','ts'),1e9)} tsSD={fx(s('small_down','ts'),1e9)} | slLU={fx(s('large_up','slope'),1e-6)} slLD={fx(s('large_down','slope'),1e-6)} "
              f"tsLU={fx(s('large_up','ts'),1e9)} tsLD={fx(s('large_down','ts'),1e9)} osLD={fx(s('large_down','os'),100)}")
    sys.stdout.flush()
    return p


if __name__ == '__main__':
    cands = json.loads(sys.argv[1])
    corners = [(c.split('/')[0], float(c.split('/')[1]) * 1e-12) for c in (sys.argv[2].split(',') if len(sys.argv) > 2 else ['N/5'])]
    for tag, over in cands.items():
        screen(tag, over, corners)
EOF

# OTA-FIXED-SKY130: electrical and measurement contract

OTA-FIXED-SKY130 is the current fixed-topology transistor sizing, bias, and compensation task.

All analyses use the pinned SKY130 combined/continuous model family. The calibrated absolute follower-error limit is **5 mV**. The registered task packet, specification JSON, initial design, bounds, testbench, evaluation matrix, and source hashes define the executable experiment. A participant's results cannot change the scoring rules for its registered campaign. Machine filenames and IDs are retained for exact hash matching.

## Circuit and public domain

Optimize the documented 14 variables of a two-stage, single-ended OTA. The input devices and load are symmetric, `nf=1`, and `IREF=10 µA`. `Cc` is 0.1–5 pF, `Rz` is 0–50 kΩ, width is at most 100 µm, length is at least 0.15 µm, and total Σ(W×L) is at most 2,000 µm². Every device must remain in the frozen OTA-FIXED-SKY130 W/L domain in `bounds.json`. The evaluator uses native ngspice and SKY130 1.8 V normal-VT NFET/PFET models. Topology changes are outside the task.

The specified load interval is continuous from 2 to 5 pF. Public exploration uses 2 and 5 pF. The nine process, supply, and temperature conditions are:

| ID | Corner | VDD | Temperature |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| N | TT | 1.80 V | 27 °C |
| SLC | SS | 1.62 V | −40 °C |
| SLH | SS | 1.62 V | 125 °C |
| SHC | SS | 1.98 V | −40 °C |
| SHH | SS | 1.98 V | 125 °C |
| FLC | FF | 1.62 V | −40 °C |
| FLH | FF | 1.62 V | 125 °C |
| FHC | FF | 1.98 V | −40 °C |
| FHH | FF | 1.98 V | 125 °C |

| Scored requirement | Limit |
|---|---:|
| Low-frequency loop gain | ≥60 dB |
| Unity-gain frequency | ≥15 MHz |
| Phase margin | ≥60° |
| Power | ≤600 µW |
| Maximum DC tracking error | ≤2 mV |
| Small-step overshoot | ≤10% |
| Large-step 20–80% slew | ≥10 V/µs |
| Small-step settling | Within 1% by 100 ns |
| Large-step settling | Within 1% by 200 ns |
| Input-referred integrated noise, 10 Hz–10 MHz | ≤100 µV RMS |
| PSRR+ at 1 kHz / 1 MHz | ≥60 dB / ≥24 dB |
| Open-loop CMRR at 1 kHz | ≥50 dB |
| Deterministic robustness pass fraction | ≥27/30 within the frozen absolute follower-error limit |

Gain margin and statistical quiescent power are characterization metrics. The absolute follower-error limit is **5 mV**, selected before independent statistical verification. Functional limits are checked under the continuous models.

## Testbench and measurements

The follower connects `vout` to `vin_n`, with `vin_p` as the non-inverting input. VSS is 0 V and VCM is 0.9 V. An ideal source injects 10 µA from ground into `ibias`, with AC magnitude zero. Participants cannot edit the PDK, loads, sources, testbench, or evaluator. Solver settings are `reltol=1e-4`, `abstol=1e-13`, `vntol=1e-9`, and `chgtol=1e-16`.

**Operating point and DC.** At each load, evaluate input levels 0.75, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0, and 1.05 V. Power is the net power entering the DUT from VDD, bias, input, and feedback ports, not VDD alone. Score the maximum over the complete DC sweep only when every required point is valid. Device operating points at the five input levels are extracted from that same sweep; they incur no extra launches. Sweep the follower from 0.75 to 1.05 V on a 5 mV grid and take the largest absolute input/output difference. Keep per-load results distinct from load-independent aggregates.

**Loop response.** Insert a series source in feedback with DC zero and AC magnitude one. Use `L = −(1 + 1/V(vin_n))`. The verification band is 10 Hz–1 GHz with 20 points per decade. All samples must be finite, frequencies strictly increasing, endpoints within relative error `1e-7`, and adjacent log10 frequency spacing at most `0.050001`. Missing values, non-finite values, or inadequate coverage are `INVALID_DATA`.

Interpolate linearly in log10 frequency and save every unity crossing, touch, and boundary event. The first valid positive-to-negative gain crossing sets UGF. Unwrap phase from the lowest-frequency principal phase, limiting each adjacent change to ±180°. Phase margin is `180° + unwrapped phase` at the selected UGF. Save phase and margin at every crossing. The stored field `phase_margin_deg_worst` contains the selected crossing's margin; its selection metadata makes that meaning explicit.

After the selected UGF, any saved gain sample above `0 dB + 1e-6 dB` fails `no_0db_recross`. Do not infer an unseen curve shape between samples. No valid UGF or non-finite PM cannot pass. Save all odd-180° phase crossings and calculate GM as negative loop gain there. Complete in-band data with no phase crossing receives `NO_PHASE_CROSSING_IN_BAND`, distinct from missing data. A phase crossing is never required merely to create a GM number.

**Transient response.** Evaluate both directions of the small step 0.8↔1.0 V and large step 0.75↔1.05 V. Hold the initial level for 100 ns, use a 1 ns edge, and observe through 2.1 µs with a maximum 25 ps output step. Measure settling from the edge start. The band is 1% of the input step and is centered on that PVT/load's independently solved destination DC output. Record the interpolated final entry that stays within the band until the end. Score DC tracking separately; a transient tail sample does not replace the DC solution. If a complete waveform stays outside the band, settling is null with the observation time saved as a lower bound; a bound past the limit is a MISS. Overshoot remains measurable even if settling fails.

Overshoot is the largest excursion past target divided by the absolute independent DC output transition. Only small-step overshoot is scored; large-step overshoot is retained as characterization. Large-step slew uses the first ordered, direction-correct 20% and 80% crossings after the edge ends. Retain later recrossings. Missing, non-finite, short, or target-less waveforms are measurement failures, not convenient electrical values.

**Noise.** Save output noise ASD referred to the input using the same circuit's input gain. Integrate its squared PSD by the trapezoid rule in frequency, interpolating band boundaries, then take the square root. The native sweep is 10 Hz–10 MHz at 50 points per decade. Keep PSD points and the integrals from 10, 100, and 1,000 Hz. The lower cutoff by itself does not establish the 1/f contribution.

**PSRR+.** In the same DC follower, measure `Hsupply` with VDD AC=1 and input AC=0, and `Hsignal` with VDD AC=0 and input AC=1. Both AC sweeps run in one ngspice process. VCM=0.9 V and IREF=10 µA do not follow supply ripple. Input-referred PSRR+ is `20 log10(|Hsignal/Hsupply|)`. Read 1 kHz and 1 MHz directly and save intermediate sweep data. Do not convert a zero transfer to an automatic infinite PASS. Score at N, SLH, and FHC with 2 and 5 pF loads.

**CMRR.** Use the natural open-loop DC operating point with both inputs fixed at **0.9 V**, independent of VDD. Differential excitation is Vin+ AC=0.5∠0° and Vin− AC=0.5∠180°, giving Vdiff=1 V. Common excitation is AC=1∠0° on both inputs, giving Vcm=1 V. Compute Ad=Vout/Vdiff, Acm=Vout/Vcm, and CMRR=20 log10(abs(Ad/Acm)) at exactly 1 kHz. Each native case bundles both AC sweeps and the DC operating point. Retain both raw AC paths, input phasors and OP data. Zero transfers, invalid OP and nonfinite values are measurement failures. Score N, SLH and FHC at 2 and 5 pF. The native 24-case calibration and independent amplitude-normalization check support 50 dB.

**Statistical follower error.** The scored deck places `.option seed` before `.lib ".../combined/continuous/sky130.lib.spice" mc`, then explicitly sets `.param MC_MM_SWITCH=1`. Use a unity-gain follower, VDD=1.80 V, 27 °C, CL=2 pF, Vin+=0.9 V and IREF=10 µA. For every sample retain convergence/status, Vout, signed `follower_error_v=Vout−0.9`, its absolute value and signed-port quiescent power. This is **closed-loop follower offset/error**, not an exact intrinsic Vos measurement. Both `MC_PR_SWITCH=1` and `MC_MM_SWITCH=1` are measured in the native deck. Following Analog Design Bench, the unmodified native model defines this deterministic test. Its process expressions are re-evaluated per reference by the pinned ngspice; a common-die global random shift has **not** been validated. The score is a fixed-seed native-model robustness test, with no silicon-yield interpretation.

Participants may explore only the four diagnostic seeds **52001–52004**. Each records one native case within the 90-minute model-call exploration window; there is no exploration case limit. CMRR exploration is available normally. Independent verification occurs only after the submitted DUT is frozen; results are withheld from the participant.

Explore mismatch-reducing geometry variants

cd /workspace/runs && cat > mmexp.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from screen import *

B = dict(BASE); B.update({'wbn': 5, 'wtl': 30, 'wp2': 100, 'lp2': 0.4, 'wld': 24, 'cc': 3e-12})


def run_variant(tag, over, pv_dc=('N', 'SLC', 'FLC', 'SLH', 'FLH', 'FHH', 'SHC')):
    p = dict(B); p.update(over)
    p, e = tune(p)
    errs = []
    for s in SEEDS:
        r = meas(p, 'mismatch', 'N', 2e-12, s)
        d = parse_mm(r)
        errs.append(None if d.get('fail') else d['err'] * 1e3)
    dcs = {}
    for pvt in pv_dc:
        r = meas(p, 'op', pvt, 2e-12)
        dcs[pvt] = r['cases'][0]['measurements']['dc_tracking_error_v_max'] * 1e3
    ok = [x for x in errs if x is not None]
    rms = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in ok) / max(1, len(ok)))
    print(f"{tag:6s} wld={p['wld']:.4g} lld={p['lld']} win={p['win']} lin={p['lin']} | mm=" + ','.join('%.2f' % x if x is not None else 'NA' for x in errs) +
          f" rms={rms:.2f} max={max(abs(x) for x in ok):.2f} | dcmax=" + ' '.join(f'{k}:{v:.2f}' for k, v in dcs.items()) + f" | err09={e:.2f}")
    sys.stdout.flush()
    return p


if __name__ == '__main__':
    cands = json.loads(sys.argv[1])
    for tag, over in cands.items():
        run_variant(tag, over)
EOF

Print DC error detail for the L=1 load variant

cat > dcdetail.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from screen import *
from mmexp import B

def detail(tag, over, pvts=('N','SLC','FLC','SLH','FLH','SHC','SHH','FHC','FHH')):
    p = dict(B); p.update(over)
    print('##', tag, ' '.join(f'{k}={p[k]:.4g}' for k in KEYS))
    for pvt in pvts:
        r = meas(p, 'op', pvt, 2e-12)
        m = r['cases'][0]['measurements']
        s = ' '.join(f"{k}:{v['tracking_error_v']*1e3:+.2f}" for k, v in m['op_points'].items())
        o = m['op_points']
        v1 = ' '.join(f"{o[k]['device_op']['M1']['VDS']:.3f}" for k in ['0.750', '0.900', '1.050'])
        nt = ' '.join(f"{o[k]['node_voltages_v']['NTL']:.3f}" for k in ['0.750', '1.050'])
        n1na = ' '.join(f"{1e3*(o[k]['node_voltages_v']['N1']-o[k]['node_voltages_v']['NA']):+.0f}" for k in ['0.750', '0.900', '1.050'])
        print(f"  {pvt:4s} P={m['quiescent_power_w_worst']*1e6:.0f} err[{s}] vds1(.75,.9,1.05)={v1} NTL(.75,1.05)={nt} N1-NA(mV)={n1na}")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    detail(sys.argv[1], json.loads(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3].split(',') if len(sys.argv) > 3 else ('N','SLC','FLC','SLH','FLH','SHC','SHH','FHC','FHH'))
EOF

Screen Rz and Cc variants at hot 5 pF corner

cd /workspace/runs && cat > scr2.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json, time
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from screen import *

C = dict(BASE); C.update({"wbn":5,"wtl":30,"wp2":100,"lp2":0.4,"wld":75.93,"lld":1.0,"cc":3e-12})

def scr(tag, over, corners=(('FLH', 5e-12),), steps=True):
    p = dict(C); p.update(over)
    t0 = time.time()
    print('##', tag, 'h=' + dhash(p), ' '.join(f'{k}={p[k]:.4g}' for k in KEYS))
    for (pvt, cl) in corners:
        r = meas(p, 'loop', pvt, cl)
        lp = parse_loop(r)
        line = f"   {pvt}/{int(cl*1e12)}p gain={fx(lp['gain'])} ugf={fx(lp['ugf'],1e-6)} pm={fx(lp['pm'])} recross={lp['recross']}"
        if steps:
            st = parse_step(meas(p, 'step', pvt, cl))
            s = lambda k, f: st[k][f]
            line += (f" | osSU={fx(s('small_up','os'),100)} osSD={fx(s('small_down','os'),100)} tsSU={fx(s('small_up','ts'),1e9)} tsSD={fx(s('small_down','ts'),1e9)}"
                     f" | slLU={fx(s('large_up','slope'),1e-6)} slLD={fx(s('large_down','slope'),1e-6)} tsLU={fx(s('large_up','ts'),1e9)} tsLD={fx(s('large_down','ts'),1e9)}")
        print(line + f"  [{time.time()-t0:.0f}s]")
        sys.stdout.flush()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    cands = json.loads(sys.argv[1])
    corners = [(c.split('/')[0], float(c.split('/')[1]) * 1e-12) for c in (sys.argv[2].split(',') if len(sys.argv) > 2 else ['FLH/5'])]
    steps = (sys.argv[3] != '0') if len(sys.argv) > 3 else True
    for tag, over in cands.items():
        scr(tag, over, corners, steps)
EOF

Check loop response across all PVTs for several Rz values

cat > loops.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/runs')
from scr2 import *
def loops(tag, over):
    p = dict(C); p.update(over)
    print('##', tag, ' '.join(f'{k}={p[k]:.4g}' for k in KEYS))
    for pvt in PVTS:
        row = []
        for cl in (2e-12, 5e-12):
            lp = parse_loop(meas(p, 'loop', pvt, cl))
            row.append(f"{int(cl*1e12)}p: ugf={fx(lp['ugf'],1e-6)} pm={fx(lp['pm'])} rc={lp['recross']}")
        print(f'  {pvt:4s} ' + ' | '.join(row))
    sys.stdout.flush()
if __name__ == '__main__':
    for tag, over in json.loads(sys.argv[1]).items():
        loops(tag, over)
EOF

atb-client submit --rationale "Final design: wbn 5/1, wtl 30/1, win 72/1.2, wld 75.93/1.0, wp2 100/0.4, wn2 90/1.2, cc 3 pF, rz 1.6 kohm (area 507.7 um^2). Baseline failed at SLC/FLC (M1 in triode, ~550 mV DC error), SLH/FLH offset (5-7 mV), ~30% small-down overshoot and 6-8 V/us large-down slew at 5 pF, and PSRR 1 kHz 44 dB at SLH. Decisions: (1) scaled the whole bias mirror (Mbn 5/1, Mtl 30/1) so M8 sinks ~186 uA and tail ~61 uA; this restores large-down slew into 5 pF and removes the N1 windup behind the overshoot, with worst-case power 519 uW (SHC) vs 600 uW. (2) Replaced the load mirror with a large, long device (75.93/1.0) sized so (W/L)7/(W/L)3 matches I7/I3, giving near-zero systematic offset (N1 ~ NA), extra M1 headroom at low VDD, and lower mirror-mismatch offset (input-referred ~1/L3). (3) M7 100/0.4 for stage-2 matching and gm7. (4) Cc 3 pF raises UGF/slew/PSRR at 1 MHz; Rz 1.6 kohm places the LHP zero for PM and overshoot. Exploration results (9 PVT x 2/5 pF, PSRR/CMRR at N/SLH/FHC, 4 diagnostic MC seeds): all spec items pass, 0 of 274 margins failing. Gain >= 73.0 dB, UGF 23.7-49.3 MHz, PM 77.4-104.6 deg, no 0 dB recross, small-step overshoot <= 2.6%, large slews >= 14.9 V/us, small settle <= 35 ns, noise 36-56 uV, DC error <= 0.96 mV, PSRR 1 kHz >= 82.6 dB, PSRR 1 MHz >= 28.2 dB, CMRR >= 75.6 dB, MC follower errors +0.64/+0.81/-0.06/-1.09 mV (limit 5 mV). Tightest margin: power at SHC (13.6%). Not tested: interior loads (3-4 pF, client rejects them; expected between the 2 and 5 pF results) and the 30 hidden robustness seeds (only the 4 diagnostic seeds were run)." 2>&1 | head -40

I submitted the final design (revision ota-8bf7624177975ffe68b9). It is frozen and no verification feedback comes back. In exploration it passed every spec item across the 9 PVTs at 2 and 5 pF. PSRR and CMRR were checked at N, SLH and FHC, plus the 4 diagnostic mismatch seeds (0 of 274 checks failing).

Final parameters:

Parameter Value
wbn / lbn 5 / 1
wtl / ltl 30 / 1
win / lin 72 / 1.2
wld / lld 75.93 / 1
wp2 / lp2 100 / 0.4
wn2 / ln2 90 / 1.2
cc 3 pF
rz 1.6 kΩ
  • Bias mirror: I scaled the whole mirror down so M8 sinks about 186 µA. That fixed the baseline's weak large-step slew and the roughly 30% small-step overshoot, with worst-case power of 519 µW against the 600 µW limit.
  • Load mirror: I made it large and long, and sized it so the first-stage output sits where the second-stage input expects. That removed the systematic offset, kept the input pair out of triode at the low-supply corners, and cut mismatch.
  • Compensation: Cc of 3 pF with Rz of 1.6 kΩ sets phase margin and overshoot.

Results against the spec:

Metric Result
Gain ≥ 73.0 dB
UGF 23.7–49.3 MHz
Phase margin 77–105°
Small-step overshoot ≤ 2.6%
Large-step slew ≥ 14.9 V/µs
Noise 36–56 µV
DC error ≤ 0.96 mV
PSRR ≥ 82.6 dB at 1 kHz, ≥ 28.2 dB at 1 MHz
CMRR ≥ 75.6 dB
Mismatch error (4 seeds) ≤ 1.1 mV (limit 5 mV)

The tightest margin is power at SHC, 13.6% below the limit.

Two things were not tested. The client rejects interior loads (3–4 pF), so I only have the 2 and 5 pF results, and I expect the metrics to fall between them. I also could not run the 30 hidden robustness seeds, only the 4 diagnostic ones.

SPICEFinal submission
* ATB OTA-SKY130 DUT -- two-stage Miller-compensated OTA on SKY130 1.8 V devices
*
* Topology: M1/M2 are the NMOS differential pair with common source ntl.
* M3 is the diode-connected PMOS load at na; M4 mirrors it into n1.  M7 is
* the PMOS second stage (source vdd, gate n1, drain vout), loaded by the
* NMOS current sink M8 (drain vout, source vss).  Diode-connected Mbn accepts
* ibias; its gate also biases the NMOS tail device Mtl and M8.
* Miller compensation connects n1 to vout through Cc and optional series Rz.
* For rz = 0, render_dut connects Cc directly to vout and omits Rz.
* The netlist below is authoritative.  docs/figures/ota-sky130.svg and the
* editable project in circuits/ota-sky130/schematic/ are derived documentation,
* not sizing or evaluation sources.
*
* Static polarity (large-signal, checked in bench tb_dc):
*   A rise on vin_p raises the M2 current and lowers the M4 current; node n1
*   falls; the PMOS M7 gate falls, so M7 sources more current and vout rises.
*   Hence vin_p is the NON-inverting input and vin_n (gate of M1, whose drain
*   is the diode-connected node na) is the inverting input of the complete
*   two-stage amplifier.
*
* SKY130 devices are PDK subcircuits, so every device below is an X instance.
* Body terminals are tied to the supply rails; there is no separate bulk port
* in P0.  The DUT contains no behavioural source.
*
.subckt atb_ota_sky130 vin_p vin_n vout vdd vss ibias
XMbn ibias ibias vss  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=1 nf=1
XMtl ntl   ibias vss  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=30 l=1 nf=1
XM1  na    vin_n ntl  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=72 l=1.2 nf=1
XM2  n1    vin_p ntl  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=72 l=1.2 nf=1
XM3  na    na    vdd  vdd sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=75.93 l=1 nf=1
XM4  n1    na    vdd  vdd sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=75.93 l=1 nf=1
XM7  vout  n1    vdd  vdd sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.4 nf=1
XM8  vout  ibias vss  vss sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=90 l=1.2 nf=1
Cc   n1    nzc    3e-12
Rz   nzc   vout   1600
.ends atb_ota_sky130
